## Normalization and absence

Every participant begins at 2025-01-01; common end is 2026-11-07 (exclusive); canonical timezone America/Chicago. The timeline shows observations on hatched unknown coverage. No empty interval is represented as verified inactivity. Earliest observed evidence is not first-ever activity.

## Discovery audit

Initial character histories seed uploader/guild identities from reports with target-name actors and encounter fights. Those collections are searched in priority-window order, then across the full requested range. The first priority window additionally receives global public daily searches in every configured zone, with configured game-zone filters when encounter-level evidence validates them. Scoped collections remain unfiltered by game zone. reportData.reports supports date, guild, uploader, zone and gameZone filters; there is no character or encounter filter. Public global discovery was validated, not assumed. API introspection and actual queries confirm the following limits:

- Character recentReports: pages 1–5.
- reportData.reports: maximum page 25 (server error observed on page 26).
- Global reports return total=-1 and last_page=-1; use has_more_pages.
- Monthly unfiltered global search exceeded page 25; the renewed run partitions the priority month into local-calendar days. A preliminary broad legacy-zone discovery was narrowed using an encounter-verified game-zone filter to avoid spending the API budget on unrelated ultimate encounters.
- Newly discovered target-linked uploader/guild collections receive one follow-up generation of priority/full-period searches; discovery is not an unbounded crawl.

- All collection exhaustion claims concern returned public search indexes, not complete real-world raid history.

| Source / scope | Start → exclusive end / zone | Reports | Pages | Incomplete |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| Character recentReports kish | Retrieved character index; 440 reported records, not exhaustive real-world coverage | 440 | 5 | False |
| Character recentReports rhaenyra | Retrieved character index; 1176 reported records, not exhaustive real-world coverage | 500 | 5 | True |
| Preserved multi-source inventory {} | Character indexes, scoped uploaders/guilds and public November discovery; not every inventoried report has participant metadata | 37072 | None | True |
| Cached report participant/fight inspection {} | Accessible cached actor lists inspected; target-name reports receive fight-level inspection. No continuous historical coverage asserted | 16972 | None | False |
| Priority shared-session uploaders {} | 100 frozen newer candidates reviewed newest-first; all lack both target identities in their rosters. This targeted scope is complete | 100 | None | False |

## Latest-status verification

5,430 later cached encounter fights and 100 additional priority uploader-linked reports were checked without finding a newer shared fight. The targeted scope has 0 reports remaining; 10,998 broader candidates remain unresolved.

Latest verified shared logged fight, not an exhaustively proven last actual instance

Counts are report/fight evidence records; different uploads can describe the same physical pull

## Coverage limitations

- Public accessible logs only; private, deleted and unlogged activity unavailable.
- Character recentReports accepts pages 1–5; reported totals are not complete historical coverage.
- Global report search returns total=-1; completeness requires has_more_pages=false, not total.
- Date filters select report collections; long reports crossing search boundaries may be missed.
- Same-name null-world actors remain candidates. No inferred aliases or server transfers.
- Unknown periods are not zero activity; no continuous raid-activity coverage asserted.
- Observed clusters group report code + local calendar date, not exact instance entry/exit.
- Duplicate report/fight keys removed; alternate uploads of one physical pull may remain distinct.
- The latest verified shared fight is November 15, 2025; the absolute last shared logged fight and last actual instance remain unproven.
- 5,430 later cached DSR fights and 100 additional priority uploader-linked reports produced no newer shared match. 10,998 broader actor-unknown candidates remain unexamined.
- Collection was deliberately bounded. Publication uses existing evidence and is not blocked on another API scan.
- Shared session counts are observed report/local-day clusters, not necessarily unique physical sessions across alternate uploads.

## Remaining questions

- Can participants provide missing-period report codes, uploader names, guild/static collections, or independently evidenced historical aliases?
- Were some sessions private, deleted, unlogged, or indexed under historical identities not exposed by the API?
- Can an authorized human inspect Tomestone and supply candidate links without bypassing its access controls?


Public report-by-report ledger, exact fight timestamps, candidate identities, discovery boundaries and evidence links: [data.json](data.json).
